## Accepted decisions and boundaries

- Validation and technical visual inspection must be unattended. Owner aesthetic
  approval is separate; there is no manual-playtest completion dependency.
- Ask before implementing any materially new rules/engine or testing subsystem.
  No omission, proxy or approximation is authorized by silence.
- Preserve released GUIDs, costs, action economy and save compatibility. No
  general performance, flight/navigation or global AI expansion.
- Freeze product/assets/compiled observers/publication inputs for qualification;
  use the existing candidate receipts and retain exact package identity.
- Preserve current human installation, all protected saves and immutable
  KMG_AUTOMATION_BASELINE. Only the authorized Working save or isolated fixture
  can be changed under the existing transaction and lease procedures.

Early proof selects A3/A4's uncertain native seams before secondary detail.
Selected first gameplay probe: `disposable-expanded-summoning-sprint32-native-review`,
`reviewPhase=satyr-control`, standalone RTWP; one process, 10-15 minutes including
staging/restoration, 600-second native deadline. Existing save-free fixture;
fresh Satyr per positive/immune-fallback case, one owned resettable hostile.
Expected evidence: native AI command, Will/DC18 and Frightened, real one-use
resource and standard action, then refusal plus a real shortbow event against
explicit native frightened immunity. No prescribed dice or issued casts/attacks.
The final sequence is scoped to this table; necessary fresh-process/profile
boundaries remain. Expected duration will be declared after fixture selection,
before dispatch. A focused ordinary selection targets 15 minutes and at most
30 minutes excluding safe restoration. Two repeated orchestration failures or
an hour without useful evidence triggers a changed approach, not a new quota.

### Subsequent bounded audit identifies an actual Courage recipient defect

The recipient-only fixture correction was committed as e7e44e59, but no package/native run was made from it. Further required contract inspection found that using an allied summon alone would miss the actual support defect. ExpandedSummoningBlueprints.SetSummonedFaction assigns the native Summoned faction; installed UnitDescriptor.IsPlayerFaction checks actual faction, and IsAlly from a nonplayer summon excludes player-faction targets. The accepted mephit support implementation explicitly documents this contract and uses ContextConditionIsEnemy with Not=true (ExpandedSummoningSpecialBuilder.cs around1590); ArchonAidEligibility uses the same nonenemy relation. Thus Lillend's unadapted native Courage IsAlly filter was a product defect for party support, not only a fixture prerequisite problem.

Correct only the private Lillend Courage area's delivery condition with that existing native support mapping. Keep native effect/area/lifecycle/actions/upkeep. The bounded regression keeps its positively allied Satyr AND requires the exact player-faction caster (IsAlly=false, IsEnemy=false) to receive actual+2 and one source-owned buff; an owned hostile inside the observed native area radius receives none. Both external recipients are captured in the existing source-grant references. No stats changed, no new subsystem. Native IsPlayerFaction/RuleSummonUnit/UnitPartSummonedMonster IL retained in `sprint32-summon-faction-contract-il.txt` and `sprint32-summoned-owner-faction-il.txt`. Requalify affected Courage support/lifecycle in RTWP and turn mode on the next single package; prior action evidence remains old execution, not fresh proof of the changed area condition.

Independent reviewer examined exact private-area diff, captured native single-IsAlly condition, accepted mephit mapping and regression/lifecycle capture. No dispatch blocker; acknowledged earlier recipient-only review missed party-support defect. Three selected fresh cases appropriate. Focused compile22.2s/tests1s PASS5/2753 before final explicit IsEnemy target guard; exact candidate build will verify final bytes. All native outcomes remain pending.
